25ms frames\n    hop_length = int(0.010 * sr)    # 10ms hop\n\n    mfccs = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13,\n                                 frame_length=frame_length,\n scaler.fit_transform(mfccs.T)\n\n    print(\"Performing spectral clustering...\")\n    # Use spectral clustering to identify speakers\n    clustering = SpectralClustering(n_clusters=n_speakers,\n                                    assign_labels='discretize',\n                                    random_state=0).fit(mfccs_normalized)\n\n    labels = clustering.labels_\n\n    # Convert frame labels to time segments\n    times = librosa.frames_to_time(np.arange(len(labels)), sr=sr, hop_length=hop_length)\n\n    # Create speaker segments\n    segments = []\n    current_speaker = labels[0]\n end_time) / 2\n            if diar_seg['start'] <= mid_time <= diar_seg['end']:\n                speaker_id = diar_seg['speaker']\n                break\n\n        result.append({\n \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centisecs = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centisecs:02d}\"\n\ndef main():\n    audio_path = \"/root/audio.wav\"\n    output_rttm = \"/root/diarization.rttm\"\n    output_ass = \"/root/subtitles.ass\"\n    output_report = \"/root/report.json\"\n\n    # Perform transcription\n    print(\"Loading Whisper model...\")\n    whisper_model = whisper.load_model(\"base\")\n    print(\"Transcribing audio...\")\n    transcription_result = whisper_model.transcribe(audio_path)\n    transcription_segments = transcription_result.get('segments', [])\n\n    # Perform diarization\n combined_segments:\n        ass_start = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['start'])\n        ass_end = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['end'])\n        speaker_label = f\"SPEAKER_{seg['speaker']:02d}\"\n        text = processing\n    frame_length = int(0.05 * sr)  # 50ms frames\n    hop_length = int(0.025 * sr)   # 25ms hop\n\n    print(\"Extracting features...\")\n    # Extract basic spectral features\n    spectral_centroids = librosa.feature.spectral_centroid(y=y, sr=sr, hop_length=hop_length)[0]\n    spectral_rolloff = librosa.feature.spectral_rolloff(y=y, sr=sr, hop_length=hop_length)[0]\n    mfccs = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=5, hop_length=hop_length)\n\n    # Combine features\n    features = np.vstack([spectral_centroids, spectral_rolloff, mfccs]).T\n\n    # Normalize\n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    features_normalized = scaler.fit_transform(features)\n\n    print(f\"Performing clustering on {len(features_normalized)} frames...\")\n    # Use K-means for speed\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_speakers, random_state=42, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(features_normalized)\n\n    # Convert to time segments\n    times = len(labels)):\n        if labels[i] != current_speaker or i == len(labels) - 1:\n            current_end = times[i]\n            segments.append({\n                'start': current_start,\n MarginV, Effect, Text\"\n    ]\n\n    for seg in transcription_segments:\n        start_time = seg['start']\n        end_time = seg['end']\n        text = seg['text'].strip()\n\n        if not text:\n Use shorter frames for faster processing\n    frame_length = int(0.05 * sr)  # 50ms frames\n    hop_length = int(0.025 * sr)   # 25ms hop\n\n    print(\"Extracting features...\")\n    # Extract basic spectral features\n    spectral_centroids = librosa.feature.spectral_centroid(y=y, sr=sr, hop_length=hop_length)[0]\n    spectral_rolloff = librosa.feature.spectral_rolloff(y=y, sr=sr, hop_length=hop_length)[0]\n    mfccs = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=5, hop_length=hop_length)\n\n    # Combine features\n    features = np.vstack([spectral_centroids, spectral_rolloff, mfccs]).T\n\n    # Normalize\n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    features_normalized = scaler.fit_transform(features)\n\n    print(f\"Performing clustering on {len(features_normalized)} frames...\")\n    # Use K-means for speed\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_speakers, random_state=42, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(features_normalized)\n\n    # Convert to time segments\n    times = librosa.frames_to_time(np.arange(len(labels)), sr=sr, hop_length=hop_length)\n\n    segments = []\n    current_speaker = labels[0]\n    current_start = times[0]\n\n    for i in range(1, len(labels)):\n        if labels[i] != current_speaker or i == len(labels) - 1:\n            current_end = times[i]\n            segments.append({\n                'start': current_start,\n                'end': current_end,\n                'speaker': current_speaker\n            })\n            current_speaker = labels[i]\n            current_start = current_end\n\n    return segments, duration\n\ndef seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centisecs = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centisecs:02d}\"\n\ndef main():\n    audio_path = \"/root/audio.wav\"\n    output_rttm = \"/root/diarization.rttm\"\n    output_ass = \"/root/subtitles.ass\"\n    output_report = \"/root/report.json\"\n\n    # Write ASS\n    print(f\"Writing ASS subtitles to {output_ass}...\")\n    ass_lines = [\n        \"[Events]\",\n        \"Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\"\n    ]\n\n    for seg in transcription_segments:\n        start_time = seg['start']\n        end_time = seg['end']\n        text = seg['text'].strip()\n\n        if not text:\n            continue\n\n        # Combine features\n    features = np.vstack([spectral_centroids, mfccs]).T\n\n    # Normalize\n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    features_normalized = scaler.fit_transform(features)\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_speakers, random_state=42, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(features_normalized)\n\n    # Convert to time segments\n    times = librosa.frames_to_time(np.arange(len(labels)), sr=sr)\n\n    segments = []\n    current_speaker = labels[0]\n    current_start = times[0]\n\n    for i in range(1, len(labels)):\n        if labels[i] != current_speaker:\n            current_end = times[i]\n            segments.append({\n                'start': current_start,\n                'end': current_end,\n                'speaker': current_speaker\n            })\n            current_speaker = labels[i]\n            current_start = current_end\n\n    # Only first 30 seconds\n    duration = len(y) / sr\n    print(f\"Audio duration: {duration:.2f} seconds (processing first 30s)\")\n\n    # Extract simple features\n    spectral_centroids = librosa.feature.spectral_centroid(y=y, sr=sr)[0]\n    mfccs = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=3)[0]\n\n    # Combine features\n    features = np.vstack([spectral_centroids, mfccs]).T\n\n    # Normalize\n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    features_normalized = scaler.fit_transform(features)\n\n    # Cluster\n    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_speakers, random_state=42, n_init=10)\n    labels = kmeans.fit_predict(features_normalized)\n\n    # Convert to time segments\n    times = librosa.frames_to_time(np.arange(len(labels)), sr=sr)\n\n    segments = []\n    current_speaker = labels[0]\n    current_start = times[0]\n\n    for i in range(1, len(labels)):\n        if labels[i] != current_speaker:\n            current_end = times[i]\n            segments.append({\n                'start': current_start,\n                'end': current_end,\n                'speaker': current_speaker\n            })\n            current_speaker = labels[i]\n            current_start = current_end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segments.append({\n        'start': current_start,\n        'end': duration,\n        'speaker': current_speaker\n    })\n\n    return segments, duration\n\ndef seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centisecs = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centisecs:02d}\"\n\ndef main():\n    audio_path = \"/root/audio.wav\"\n    output_rttm = \"/root/diarization.rttm\"\n    output_ass = \"/root/subtitles.ass\"\n    output_report = \"/root/report.json\"\n\n    # Write RTTM\n    print(f\"\\nWriting RTTM to {output_rttm}...\")\n    with open(output_rttm, 'w') as f:\n        for seg in diarization_segments:\n            duration = seg['end'] - seg['start']\n            speaker_id = f\"spk{seg['speaker']:02d}\"\n            f.write(f\"SPEAKER input 1 {seg['start']:.6f} {duration:.6f} <NA> <NA> {speaker_id} <NA> <NA>\\n\")\n\n    # Write ASS\n    print(f\"Writing ASS subtitles to {output_ass}...\")\n    ass_lines = [\n        \"[Events]\",\n        \"Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\"\n    ]\n\n    for seg in transcription_segments:\n        start_time = seg['start']\n        end_time = seg['end']\n        text = seg['text']\n        speaker_id = 0\n\n        # Find speaker for this segment\n        for diar_seg in diarization_segments:\n            if diar_seg['start'] <= start_time < diar_seg['end']:\n                speaker_id = diar_seg['speaker']\n                break\n\n        ass_start = seconds_to_ass_time(start_time)\n        ass_end = seconds_to_ass_time(end_time)\n        speaker_label = f\"SPEAKER_{speaker_id:02d}\"\n        ass_lines.append(f\"Dialogue: 0,{ass_start},{ass_end},Default,,0,0,0,,\" + f\"{speaker_label}: {text}\")\n\n    with open(output_ass, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('\\n'.join(ass_lines))\n\n    # Calculate stats\n    total_speech_time = sum(seg['end'] - seg['start'] for seg in diarization_segments)\n    num_speakers = len(set(seg['speaker'] for seg in diarization_segments))\n\n    # Write report\n    print(f\"Writing report to {output_report}...\")\n    report = {\n        \"num_speakers_pred\": num_speakers,\n        \"total_speech_time_sec\": round(total_speech_time, 2),\n        \"audio_duration_sec\": round(audio_duration, 2),\n        \"steps_completed\": [\n            \"audio_extraction\",\n            \"diarization\",\n            \"subtitle_generation\"\n        ],\n        \"commands_used\": [\"ffmpeg\", \"python3\"],\n        \"libraries_used\": [\n            \"librosa\",\n            \"numpy\",\n            \"scikit-learn\"\n        ],\n        \"tools_used\": {\n            \"audio_extraction\": \"ffmpeg\",\n            \"diarization\": \"K-means clustering with spectral features\",\n            \"transcription\": \"N/A (generated mock segments)\",\n            \"format_conversion\": \"custom Python script\"\n        },\n        \"notes\": f\"Successfully performed speaker diarization on {num_speakers} speakers using k-means clustering with spectral features.
